## How to use this file

- IDs are permanent. Never renumber or reuse an ID; append new ones.
- `[ ]` means not proven. `[x]` means complete with linked/reproducible proof.
- Track the single active slice in “Current work” below; do not invent a third checkbox state.
- A code commit is not proof by itself. Proof is a test, artifact, screenshot, report, fixture result, or reviewed document.
- If an item changes an Accepted decision, stop, write an ADR, and update every affected doc/checklist item in the same commit.
- If blocked, leave the item unchecked and add a dated entry in the blocker log with the next action.
- Do not close a milestone gate until all required child items and its end-to-end journey pass.
- Optional items are labeled **Optional**; all others are release requirements for their milestone.
- Security, privacy, accessibility, migration, and recovery work is part of the feature, not a final cleanup phase.

Four focused unit tests, both real held-response tests, the full nine-test extension suite and 18 repeated executions pass. The complete local gate passes. Fresh clean-commit hosted verification remains; see [routing lifetime proof](../../proof/extension-routing-lifetime-verification.md). |
| Windows harness verification (2026-10-19) | The actual PowerShell regression reproduced the invalid exception type, then passed exited-child sampling/cleanup, live-root measurement, executable confinement and non-exit-error propagation after the repair. All six focused packaging tests and the complete local gate pass. CI explicitly runs the Windows-only regression before installed startup proof. Clean-commit Windows job `118024695770` at `2ed31b8` passed the regression, installed startup/resources and package-proof upload in run `39241911597`; the aggregate CI job is still running; see [process-race proof](../../proof/native-package-process-race-verification.md). No product or Accepted decision changes. |
| Unseeded submission finding (2026-10-19) | The Mark Applied review lists only existing active Applied-category stages (`application-submission-repository.ts`), while the current new-vault creation path creates only an explicitly user-named Preparing stage. The canonical test supplies its Applied stage through the reference fixture. Add a normal-user explicitly named Applied-stage creation path before removing that seed; do not invent default display stages or close `Q-006`. |
| Requirement-source verification (2026-10-19) | Saved-job proposal parsing, source-bound atomic acceptance and explicit ephemeral rejection are implemented. The unseeded built-PWA offline journey passes exact line/category/provenance, duplicate/forged identity rejection, unchanged source, reload, axe and 320-pixel checks. Full local gate passes, including shared browser/native source-acceptance assertions; final unit matrix is 127 files / 996 tests. Clean-commit hosted evidence remains pending; see [source-review proof](../../proof/stored-requirement-source-verification.md). `Q3-005` remains open. |
| Requirement-source checkpoint scope (2026-10-19) | Connect real saved-job Requirements to bounded deterministic proposals from immutable retained snapshot text. Resolve proposal identities again at acceptance, atomically persist exact line provenance and the confirmed requirement, prevent repeated acceptance, and keep rejection ephemeral as MAT-002 specifies. Prove no parse/reject writes, reload continuity, forged/wrong-job rejection, offline behavior and reflow. No dependency, schema, or Accepted decision change is planned. |
| Active stored-Pipeline slice (2026-10-19) | Compose real stored jobs into normal Pipeline navigation, preserving the synthetic catalog as a separate proof surface. The first prerequisite is the missing concrete SQLite adapter for the existing `APP-005` query port: counts, configured stage/unassigned groups, stable keyset pages and complete job-workspace details. Prove archive/status scope, cursor invalidation, real relationships and read-only behavior across adapters before shell wiring. Do not seed defaults, replace SQLite truth, weaken mutations into React-only state, or close `Q3-005` based on deep-linked fixture journeys. No migration, dependency or Accepted decision changes are planned. |
| Navigation/reflow verification (2026-10-19) | Save-before-route coordination, generation-bound editor loads, unsaved-unload warning and single-flight autosave are implemented. Twelve focused tests and the unseeded built-PWA offline journey pass, including real Reload cancellation, held/failed saves, retained Back/Forward history, search, reload and abandoned-load handling. Hosted copy run `39234126552` failed on intrinsic history-selector overflow; a 449-pixel control / 478-pixel page reproduction at a 320-pixel viewport now passes with shrinkable grid/control CSS and a stronger font-metrics regression. Full local gate passes with 126 files / 987 unit tests, eight resilience journeys and browser/native recovery; fresh hosted verification remains; see [navigation proof](../../proof/document-navigation-verification.md). |
| Derivative checkpoint scope (2026-10-19) | Create a job-specific resume/cover-letter copy from an explicitly chosen immutable reusable version, without selecting or submitting it. Add immutable exact-version origin metadata (existing lineage records only the base document ID), expose that origin in the editor, include it in human-readable export and browser/native recovery, and prove stale/submitted/invalid targets and partial writes fail closed. Existing legacy derivatives retain unknown version origin rather than receiving invented backfill. |
| Derivative verification progress (2026-10-19) | Command, transactional repository, immutable origin schema, production stored-job form and editor-origin display are implemented. The unseeded built-PWA offline journey passes for both resume and cover-letter copies with exact IR/hash/reload identity, unchanged application selections, axe and 320-pixel proof. Full local gate passes with 125 files / 979 unit tests, shared exact-origin contracts in Node/browser/native SQLite, and clean browser/native schema-158 origin recovery. Hosted verification remains; see [copy proof](../../proof/job-document-derivation-verification.md). The previous pushed attempt/OPFS checkpoint `db909da` is fully green in hosted run `39229249818`. |
| Application-attempt checkpoint (2026-10-19) | Start preparation locally now creates an actual attempt, user-named Preparing stage when needed, job projection, and timeline event atomically. Unseeded manual-capture → review → save job → start preparation → reload passes offline, including duplicate rejection, no document auto-selection, no submission, axe, and 320-pixel proof. Full local gate and 124-file/949-test unit matrix pass. The prior checkpoint's Chrome 152 failure exposed an OPFS startup-handle release race; a narrowly bounded fresh-open repair and real-worker single-write regression pass locally. Hosted verification remains; see [attempt/startup proof](../../proof/application-attempt-creation-verification.md). |
| Production creation slice (2026-10-19) | Normal Documents creation now writes a manually authored reusable resume or cover letter, base lineage, and first immutable version in one transaction. Unseeded built-PWA offline proof covers input retention on failure, blank/text versions, exact hash/reload identity, axe, and 320-pixel reflow. Full local gate passes, followed by final 122-file/925-test unit and proof-retention checks; hosted verification of this new slice remains. See [manual creation proof](../../proof/manual-document-creation-verification.md). Job derivatives and application-attempt creation remain subsequent work; `Q3-005` stays open. |
| Hosted regression follow-up (2026-10-19) | CI run `39221886529` exposed a calendar-dependent extension adversarial fixture: its fixed September 27 capture has expired against the real service-worker clock. The repaired fixture retains its actual capture instant and proves an expired draft is rejected without queuing. All seven extension tests, the full local gate, and every required hosted job passed at `752a168` in run `39223465958`. No production expiry policy changed; see [clock regression proof](../../proof/extension-clock-regression-verification.md). |
| Integration finding (2026-10-19) | The Requirements demonstration currently mutates React state while template generation reads SQLite selections. Connect canonical stored-job routes to the existing durable requirement/evidence operations and verify reload continuity before treating the complete journey as proven. Final closure CI at `a58b57f` passed in run `36732872523`. |
| Local checkpoint (2026-10-19) | Stored-job requirement review commits to SQLite and feeds the template engine. The scaffolded production-PWA journey passes offline, including stale-write rejection, exact DOCX hash retention, immutable submission reload, axe, and 320-pixel reflow. Full local and hosted verification at `752a168` passes after [six dependency security fixes](../../proof/dependency-security-maintenance-2026-10-19.md). New reusable-document creation is proven separately above; production application-attempt and job-derivative/document-set creation remain. |
| Expected proof | One production E2E journey starts with a synthetic local resume import, explicitly reviews/confirms evidence without overwriting prior user truth, compares that evidence with a synthetic job, prepares exact versioned application documents with AI disabled, exports locally, marks Applied without submitting, reloads, and retrieves the immutable submitted set and retained artifact identities. The artifact must prove provenance, exact version/hash continuity, accountless/offline/zero-egress behavior, accessible named actions, narrow-screen reflow, and durable recovery through SQLite rather than test-only state. |
| Blocker | None for a repository-backed synthetic canonical journey. Representative human terminology/action validation remains separately blocked under `Q3-002`; real resumes or applicant data must not be committed as fixtures. Exact reference-device assistive-technology rows remain open under `Q1-003`/Phase 6. `Q2-001` still requires representative human usability sessions, and `Q2-003` retains manual keyboard/screen-reader/mobile evidence beyond automation. Actual extension publication/hosted transfer remains blocked on the public origin, hosting target, release identities/accounts, and owner-supplied public details. `PEX-006` remains open until a reviewed extension-specific adapter genuinely needs persistent host access. `GATE-2`, `GATE-1`, the participant study, and `FND-001` remain independently blocked. |
| Next handoff | Inspect exact hosted proof of the locally verified canonical task-header checkpoint after commit/push. Continue independent task feedback/undo composition under accepted command/draft contracts while awaiting ADR-0009 owner acceptance; do not adopt the proposed converter or silently substitute a library. Normal creation form and complete scheduled/lifetime/reflow journeys remain required. Do not shift clock times, infer an overlap from device zone or replace the workflow with UTC-only input. Remaining task lifecycle, interviews/reminders, writable details, Network and normal backup/recovery still need proof. `Q3-005` stays complete at `9ffc13e`; `GATE-3` and independent human/manual/reference-device/publication gates remain open. The overall goal is not complete. |

## Release decision checklist

Before any tagged release, answer in its release notes:

- [ ] Which schema, archive, capture, prompt, and sync contract versions are included?
- [ ] Which migrations run, and has restore been tested from every supported prior version?
- [ ] Which browsers/OSs and extension versions are supported by evidence?
- [ ] Did permissions, CSP, dependencies, licenses, secrets, and artifacts pass review?
- [ ] Did offline/template-only operation regress?
- [ ] Did any provider/source terms, retention, attribution, credentials, or endpoints change?
- [ ] Did any AI prompt/model/template change pass its evaluation gate?
- [ ] Are user-visible network/data-flow disclosures still accurate?
- [ ] Can users export, restore, and delete data without the hosted service?
- [ ] Are known limitations and rollback/recovery instructions published?

## First Codex work request

Use this when implementation begins:

> Start Phase 0 with `FND-001` through `FND-008`. Read `AGENTS.md`, `GOAL.md`, `10-technology-stack.md`, `11-decision-register.md`, and the relevant architecture/security documents before editing. Create only the repository/governance/build skeleton; do not implement product features. Run clean-install, typecheck, lint, and test proof. Update Current work and each completed checkbox with concrete evidence. If a selected tool has a material incompatibility, record an ADR proposal rather than silently substituting it.
